SHARED CART BADGE (localStorage 'cartCount')\n16:      ======================================================================== */\n17:   function getCartCount() {\n18:     try {\n19:       const raw = localStorage.getItem('cartCount');\n20:       const parsed = parseInt(raw, 10);\n21:       return Number.isFinite(parsed) && parsed >= 0 ? parsed : 0;\n22:     } catch (err) {\n23:       return 0;\n24:     }\n25:   }\n26: \n27:   function renderCartBadge() {\n28:     const badges = document.querySelectorAll('[data-cart-badge]');\n29:     const count = getCartCount();\n30:     badges.forEach(function (badge) {\n31:       badge.textContent = String(count);\n32:       badge.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Cart items: ' + count);\n33:     });\n34:   }\n35: \n36:   function initCartBadge() {\n37:     renderCartBadge();\n38:     window.addEventListener('storage', function (event) {\n39:       if (event.key === 'cartCount') {\n40:         renderCartBadge();\n41:       }\n42:     });\n43:   }\n44: \n45:   /* ========================================================================\n46:      2. SHARED NAVBAR SEARCH BAR\n47:      ======================================================================== */\n48:   function showToast(message) {\n49:     const existing = document.getElementById('udSharedToast');\n50:     if (existing) existing.remove();\n51: \n52:     const toast = document.createElement('div');\n53:     toast.id = 'udSharedToast';\n54:     toast.className = 'ud-toast';\n55:     toast.setAttribute('role', 'status');\n56:     toast.textContent = message;\n57:     document.body.appendChild(toast);\n58: \n59:     window.setTimeout(function () {\n60:       if (toast.parentNode) toast.remove();\n61:     }, 3500);\n62:   }\n63: \n64:   function initNavbarSearch() {\n65:     const searchForm = document.getElementById('udNavSearchForm');\n66:     const searchInput = document.getElementById('udNavSearchInput');\n67:     if (!searchForm || !searchInput) return;\n68: \n69:     searchForm.addEventListener('submit', function (event) {\n70:       event.preventDefault();\n71:       const query = searchInput.value.trim();\n72:       if (!query) {\n73:         searchInput.focus();\n74:         showToast('Enter a keyword to search URBANDROPS.');\n75:         return;\n76:       }\n77: \n78:       // If on FAQ page, filter FAQ items directly\n79:       const faqItems = document.querySelectorAll('.ud-faq-item');\n80:       if (faqItems.length > 0) {\n81:         const lower = query.toLowerCase();\n82:         let matchCount = 0;\n83:         faqItems.forEach(function (item) {\n84:           const text = item.textContent.toLowerCase();\n85:           const matched = text.includes(lower);\n86:           item.style.display = matched ? '' : 'none';\n87:           if (matched) matchCount += 1;\n88:         });\n89: \n90:         // Reveal/hide section wrappers based on visible items\n91:         document.querySelectorAll('[data-faq-section]').forEach(function (section) {\n92:           const visibleInSection = Array.from(section.querySelectorAll('.ud-faq-item')).some(function (el) {\n93:             return el.style.display !== 'none';\n94:           });\n95:           section.style.display = visibleInSection ? '' : 'none';\n96:         });\n97: \n98:         const faqEmpty = document.getElementById('faqEmptyState');\n99:         if (faqEmpty) {\n100:           faqEmpty.classList.toggle('d-none', matchCount > 0);\n101:         }\n102:         showToast(matchCount + ' FAQ result(s) for \"' + query + '\".');\n103:         return;\n104:       }\n105: \n106:       // If on Store Locator page, match city or store name\n107:       const cityFilter = document.getElementById('cityFilter');\n108:       if (cityFilter) {\n109:         const lower = query.toLowerCase();\n110:         const cities = ['islamabad', 'lahore', 'karachi'];\n111:         const matchedCity = cities.find(function (c) {\n112:           return c.includes(lower) || lower.includes(c);\n113:         });\n114:         if (matchedCity) {\n115:           const properCity = matchedCity.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + matchedCity.slice(1);\n116:           cityFilter.value = properCity;\n117:           cityFilter.dispatchEvent(new Event('change'));\n118:           showToast('Filtered stores for ' + properCity + '.');\n119:           return;\n120:         }\n121:       }\n122: \n123:       showToast('Searching catalog for \"' + query + '\"...');\n124:     });\n125:   }\n126: \n127:   /* ========================================================================\n128:      3. faqSearchInput.value.trim().toLowerCase() : '';\n      let visibleCount = 0;\n\n      sections.forEach(function (section) {\n        const sectionKey = section.getAttribute('data-faq-section');\n        const categoryMatch = activeCategory === 'all' || activeCategory === sectionKey;\n        const sectionItems = Array.from(section.querySelectorAll('.ud-faq-item'));\n        let sectionVisibleCount = 0;\n\n        sectionItems.forEach(function (item) {\n          const keywordMatch = query === '' || item.textContent.toLowerCase().includes(query);\n          const shouldShow = categoryMatch && keywordMatch;\n          item.style.display = shouldShow ? '' : 'none';\n          if (shouldShow) {\n            sectionVisibleCount += 1;\n            visibleCount += 1;\n          }\n        });\n\n        section.style.display = sectionVisibleCount > 0 ? '' : 'none';\n      });\n\n      if (faqEmpty) {\n        faqEmpty.classList.toggle('d-none', visibleCount > 0);\n      }\n\n      if (faqSearchClear) {\n        faqSearchClear.classList.toggle('d-none', query.length === 0);\n      }\n\n      if (faqSearchCount) {\n        faqSearchCount.textContent =\n          'Showing ' + visibleCount + ' of ' + allFaqItems.length + ' ' + (allFaqItems.length === 1 ? 'Question' : 'Questions');\n      }\n    }\n\n    if (faqSearchInput) {\n      faqSearchInput.addEventListener('input', applyFaqFilters);\n    }\n\n    if (faqSearchClear) {\n      faqSearchClear.addEventListener('click', function () {\n        if (faqSearchInput) {\n          faqSearchInput.value = '';\n          faqSearchInput.focus();\n        }\n        const navSearchInput = document.getElementById('udNavSearchInput');\n        if (navSearchInput) navSearchInput.value = '';\n        applyFaqFilters();\n      });\n    }\n\n    categoryButtons.forEach(function (btn) {\n      btn.addEventListener('click', function () {\n        activeCategory = btn.getAttribute('data-faq-filter') || 'all';\n\n        categoryButtons.forEach(function (b) {\n          b.classList.toggle('active', b === btn);\n        });\n\n        applyFaqFilters();\n      });\n    });\n\n    if (resetFaqSearchBtn) {\n      resetFaqSearchBtn.addEventListener('click', function () {\n        activeCategory = 'all';\n        categoryButtons.forEach(function (b) {\n          b.classList.toggle('active', b.getAttribute('data-faq-filter') === 'all');\n        });\n        if (faqSearchInput) {\n          faqSearchInput.value = '';\n          faqSearchInput.focus();\n        }\n        const navSearchInput = document.getElementById('udNavSearchInput');\n        if (navSearchInput) navSearchInput.value = '';\n        applyFaqFilters();\n      });\n    }\n\n    applyFaqFilters();\n  }\n\n  /* ========================================================================\n     5. 'false' : 'true');\n\n      if (isValid) {\n        input.classList.add('is-valid-ud');\n        if (message) feedbackEl.classList.add('is-valid');\n      } else {\n        input.classList.add('is-invalid-ud');\n        if (message) feedbackEl.classList.add('is-invalid');\n      }\n      feedbackEl.textContent = message;\n    }\n\n    function validateEmailRealtime() {\n      const val = emailInput.value.trim();\n      if (val.length === 0) {\n        setFieldFeedback(emailInput, emailFeedback, false, 'Email address is required (e.g., user@domain.com).');\n        return false;\n      }\n      if (!EMAIL_REGEX.test(val)) {\n        setFieldFeedback(emailInput, emailFeedback, false, 'Invalid email format. Use user@domain.com.');\n        return false;\n      }\n      setFieldFeedback(emailInput, emailFeedback, true, 'Valid email format.');\n      return true;\n    }\n\n    function validateName() {\n      const val = nameInput.value.trim();\n      if (val.length < 2) {\n        setFieldFeedback(nameInput, nameFeedback, false, 'Full name is required (at least 2 characters).');\n        return false;\n      }\n      setFieldFeedback(nameInput, nameFeedback, true, 'Looks good.');\n      return true;\n    }\n\n    function validateSubject() {\n      if (!subjectSelect.value) {\n        setFieldFeedback(subjectSelect, subjectFeedback, false, 'Please select a support category.');\n        return false;\n      }\n      setFieldFeedback(subjectSelect, subjectFeedback, true, 'Category ready.');\n      return true;\n    }\n\n    function renderTicketConfirmationCard(ticketCode, customerName, customerEmail, subjectText) {\n      if (!confirmationContainer) return;\n\n      confirmationContainer.innerHTML = '';\n\n      const card = document.createElement('div');\n      card.className = 'ud-ticket-confirmation-card';\n      card.setAttribute('role', 'alert');\n\n      const topRow = document.createElement('div');\n      topRow.className = 'd-flex justify-content-between align-items-start gap-3';\n\n      const headerContent = document.createElement('div');\n\n      const badge = document.createElement('span');\n      badge.className = 'ud-ticket-badge mb-2';\n      badge.textContent = 'CONFIRMED // SUPPORT LOGGED';\n\n      const heading = document.createElement('p');\n      lead.className = 'mb-0 small fw-medium';\n      lead.textContent = 'Confirmation routed to ' + customerEmail + '. Average concierge response is under 12 hours.';\n\n      headerContent.appendChild(badge);\n      headerContent.appendChild(heading);\n      headerContent.appendChild(lead);\n\n      const closeBtn = document.createElement('button');\n      closeBtn.type = 'button';\n      closeBtn.className = 'btn-close';\n      closeBtn.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Dismiss ticket confirmation');\n      closeBtn.addEventListener('click', function () {\n        confirmationContainer.classList.add('d-none');\n        confirmationContainer.innerHTML = '';\n      });\n\n      topRow.appendChild(headerContent);\n      topRow.appendChild(closeBtn);\n\n      const meta = document.createElement('div');\n      meta.className = 'ud-ticket-meta ud-mono d-flex flex-wrap gap-3';\n\n      const nameMeta = document.createElement('span');\n      nameMeta.textContent = 'REQUESTER: ' + customerName;\n\n      const subjectMeta = document.createElement('span');\n      subjectMeta.textContent = 'CATEGORY: ' + subjectText;\n\n      meta.appendChild(nameMeta);\n      meta.appendChild(subjectMeta);\n\n      card.appendChild(topRow);\n      card.appendChild(meta);\n\n      confirmationContainer.appendChild(card);\n      confirmationContainer.classList.remove('d-none');\n      confirmationContainer.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'nearest' });\n    }\n\n    // Live validation across all form fields as user types/selects\n    emailInput.addEventListener('input', validateEmailRealtime);\n    emailInput.addEventListener('blur', validateEmailRealtime);\n\n    nameInput.addEventListener('input', validateName);\n    nameInput.addEventListener('blur', validateName);\n\n    subjectSelect.addEventListener('change', validateSubject);\n    subjectSelect.addEventListener('blur', validateSubject);\n\n    messageInput.addEventListener('input', function () {\n      if (charCounter) {\n        charCounter.textContent = {\n      event.preventDefault();\n\n      const isNameOk = validateName();\n      const isEmailOk = validateEmailRealtime();\n      const isSubjectOk = validateSubject();\n      const isMessageOk = validateMessage();\n\n      if (!isNameOk || !isEmailOk || !isSubjectOk || !isMessageOk) {\n        if (confirmationContainer) {\n          confirmationContainer.classList.add('d-none');\n        }\n document.getElementById('messageCharCount');\n\n    const EMAIL_REGEX = /^[^\\s@]+@[^\\s@]+\\.[^\\s@]{2,}$/;\n\n    function setFieldFeedback(input, feedbackEl, isValid, message) {\n      input.classList.remove('is-valid-ud', 'is-invalid-ud');\n      feedbackEl.classList.remove('is-valid', 'is-invalid');\n\n      if (message === '') {\n        feedbackEl.textContent = '';\n        return;\n      }\n\n      if (isValid) {\n        input.classList.add('is-valid-ud');\n        feedbackEl.classList.add('is-valid');\n      } else {\n        input.classList.add('is-invalid-ud');\n        feedbackEl.classList.add('is-invalid');\n      }\n      feedbackEl.textContent = message;\n    }\n\n    function validateEmailRealtime() {\n      const val = emailInput.value.trim();\n      if setFieldFeedback(emailInput, emailFeedback, false, 'Enter a valid email format (e.g., alex@domain.com).');\n        return false;\n      }\n      setFieldFeedback(emailInput, emailFeedback, true, messageInput.addEventListener('input', function () {\n      if (charCounter) {\n        charCounter.textContent = messageInput.value.length + ' / 1000';\n      }\n      if (messageInput.classList.contains('is-invalid-ud')) {\n        validateMessage();\n      }\n    });\n\n    form.addEventListener('submit', function (event) {\n      event.preventDefault();\n\n      const isNameOk = validateName();\n      const isEmailOk = validateEmailRealtime();\n      const isSubjectOk = validateSubject();\n      const isMessageOk = validateMessage();\n\n      if (!isNameOk || {"step_index":46,"source":"MODEL","type":"PLANNER_RESPONSE","status":"DONE","created_at":"2026-11-27T14:21:05Z","model":"gemini-4-hydrogen-high","input_tokens":2415,"cache_read_tokens":73201,"output_tokens":1256,"content":"Refined the URBANDROPS support pages, shared styles, and JavaScript interactions across [contact-support.html](file:///c:/Users/laptop%20villa/SE-B_Group05_URBANDROP/pages/contact-support.html), [store-locator.html](file:///c:/Users/laptop%20villa/SE-B_Group05_URBANDROP/pages/store-locator.html), [faq.html](file:///c:/Users/laptop%20villa/SE-B_Group05_URBANDROP/pages/faq.html), [support.css](file:///c:/Users/laptop%20villa/SE-B_Group05_URBANDROP/css/support.css), and [support.js](file:///c:/Users/laptop%20villa/SE-B_Group05_URBANDROP/js/support.js).\n\n### Summary of Changes\n\n1. **Contact Support Live Validation & Volt Lime Ticket Card ([contact-support.html](file:///c:/Users/laptop%20villa/SE-B_Group05_URBANDROP/pages/contact-support.html), [support.css](file:///c:/Users/laptop%20villa/SE-B_Group05_URBANDROP/css/support.css#L407-L439), [support.js](file:///c:/Users/laptop%20villa/SE-B_Group05_URBANDROP/js/support.js#L486-L654))**\n   - Updated `initContactSupport()` to run live validation on `input`, `change`, and `blur` across `#supportName`, `#supportEmail` (matching `user@domain.com` via `EMAIL_REGEX`), `#supportSubject`, and `#supportMessage`.\n   - Styled `.is-invalid-ud` and `.ud-validation-msg.is-invalid` in `#FF3B30` (`--ud-danger`) so invalid fields show a red border, red focus ring, and red inline error message.\n   - Replaced the static confirmation alert with `#ticketConfirmationContainer`, where `renderTicketConfirmationCard()` dynamically generates a Volt Lime (`#CCFF00`) `.ud-ticket-confirmation-card` displaying `Ticket #SUP-XXX Created Successfully` along with requester name, email, category, and a dismiss button.\n\n2. **Store Locator \"Get Directions\" Modal ([store-locator.html](file:///c:/Users/laptop%20villa/SE-B_Group05_URBANDROP/pages/store-locator.html#L190-L243), [support.css](file:///c:/Users/laptop%20villa/SE-B_Group05_URBANDROP/css/support.css#L661-L681), [support.js](file:///c:/Users/laptop%20villa/SE-B_Group05_URBANDROP/js/support.js#L230-L353))**\n   - Added a `Get Directions` button (`data-directions-store`) to every rendered store card in `initStoreLocator()`.\n   - Added Bootstrap modal `#storeDirectionsModal` in [store-locator.html](file:///c:/Users/laptop%20villa/SE-B_Group05_URBANDROP/pages/store-locator.html#L190-L243) and `openDirectionsModal()` in [support.js](file:///c:/Users/laptop%20villa/SE-B_Group05_URBANDROP/js/support.js#L246-L280) to populate store details, pop-up & store operating hours (`#modalStoreHours`), and Google Maps direction links (`#modalDirectionsLink` for turn-by-turn directions and `#modalMapsPinLink` for location pin search), while syncing map selection.\n\n3. **FAQ Live Keyword Search ([faq.html](file:///c:/Users/laptop%20villa/SE-B_Group05_URBANDROP/pages/faq.html#L105-L132), [support.css](file:///c:/Users/laptop%20villa/SE-B_Group05_URBANDROP/css/support.css#L626-L659), [support.js](file:///c:/Users/laptop%20villa/SE-B_Group05_URBANDROP/js/support.js#L387-L480))**\n   - Added `#faqSearchInput`, `#faqSearchClear`, and `#faqSearchCount` above the FAQ category pills.\n   - Added `applyFaqFilters()` in `initFaqAccordion()` so typing in `#faqSearchInput` filters `.ud-faq-item` elements dynamically on every `input` event, respects the active category filter (`All Topics` / `Shipping` / `Returns` / `Sizing`), hides empty sections, toggles `#faqEmptyState`, and keeps `#udNavSearchForm` synced.\n\n### Verification\n- Verified via PowerShell static checks that `js/support.js` has balanced delimiters (`105` braces, `393` parentheses) and all required DOM IDs and JavaScript functions exist across the three HTML pages and `js/support.js`.\n- Note: `node` and `python` were unavailable in this environment, so live browser/DOM execution was not run here; opening the three pages in a browser will let you exercise the interactive form, modal, and search directly."}
